    id("com.android.application")

    id("dev.flutter.flutter-gradle-plugin")

android {
    namespace = "com.example.sihama"
    compileSdk = flutter.compileSdkVersion
    ndkVersion = flutter.ndkVersion

    compileOptions {
        sourceCompatibility = JavaVersion.VERSION_17
        targetCompatibility = JavaVersion.VERSION_17
    }

    defaultConfig {
        // TODO: Specify your own unique Application ID (https://developer.android.com/studio/build/application-id.html).
        applicationId = "com.example.sihama"
        // You can update the following values to match your application needs.
        // For more information, see: https://flutter.dev/to/review-gradle-config.
        minSdk = flutter.minSdkVersion
        targetSdk = flutter.targetSdkVersion
        // Uses the version code from pubspec.yaml. When using split APKs, 1000 * ABI_VERSION
        // is added automatically by Flutter. (https://developer.android.com/studio/build/configure-apk-splits#configure-APK-versions)
        // You can force using the value of versionCode by specifying the `-P force-version-code-ignoring-abi=true`
        // flag during build.
        versionCode = flutter.versionCode
        versionName = flutter.versionName
    }

    buildTypes {
        release {
            // TODO: Add your own signing config for the release build.
            // Signing with the debug keys for now, so `flutter run --release` works.
            signingConfig = signingConfigs.getByName("debug")
        }
    }
}

kotlin {
    compilerOptions {
        jvmTarget = org.jetbrains.kotlin.gradle.dsl.JvmTarget.JVM_17
    }
}

flutter {
    source = "../.."
}

<manifest xmlns:android="http://schemas.android.com/apk/res/android">
    <application
        android:label="sihama"
        android:name="${applicationName}"
        android:icon="@mipmap/ic_launcher">
        <activity
            android:name=".MainActivity"
            android:exported="true"
            android:launchMode="singleTop"
            android:taskAffinity=""
            android:theme="@style/LaunchTheme"
            android:configChanges="orientation|keyboardHidden|keyboard|screenSize|smallestScreenSize|locale|layoutDirection|fontScale|screenLayout|density|uiMode"
            android:hardwareAccelerated="true"
            android:windowSoftInputMode="adjustResize">
            <!-- Specifies an Android theme to apply to this Activity as soon as
                 the Android process has started. This theme is visible to the user
                 while the Flutter UI initializes. After that, this theme continues
                 to determine the Window background behind the Flutter UI. -->
            <meta-data
              android:name="io.flutter.embedding.android.NormalTheme"
              android:resource="@style/NormalTheme"
              />
            <intent-filter>
                <action android:name="android.intent.action.MAIN"/>
                <category android:name="android.intent.category.LAUNCHER"/>
            </intent-filter>
        </activity>
        <!-- Don't delete the meta-data below.
             This is used by the Flutter tool to generate GeneratedPluginRegistrant.java -->
        <meta-data
            android:name="flutterEmbedding"
            android:value="2" />
    </application>
    <!-- Required to query activities that can process text, see:
         https://developer.android.com/training/package-visibility and
         https://developer.android.com/reference/android/content/Intent#ACTION_PROCESS_TEXT.

         In particular, this is used by the Flutter engine in io.flutter.plugin.text.ProcessTextPlugin. -->
    <queries>
        <intent>
            <action android:name="android.intent.action.PROCESS_TEXT"/>
            <data android:mimeType="text/plain"/>
        </intent>
    </queries>
</manifest>

FILE: android\build.gradle.kts
====================================================================================================
allprojects {
    repositories {
        google()
        mavenCentral()
    }
}

val newBuildDir: Directory =
    rootProject.layout.buildDirectory
        .dir("../../build")
        .get()
rootProject.layout.buildDirectory.value(newBuildDir)

subprojects {
    val newSubprojectBuildDir: Directory = newBuildDir.dir(project.name)
    project.layout.buildDirectory.value(newSubprojectBuildDir)
}
subprojects {
    project.evaluationDependsOn(":app")
}

tasks.register<Delete>("clean") {
    delete(rootProject.layout.buildDirectory)
}

FILE: android\settings.gradle.kts
====================================================================================================
pluginManagement {
    val flutterSdkPath =
        run {
            val properties = java.util.Properties()
            file("local.properties").inputStream().use { properties.load(it) }
            val flutterSdkPath = properties.getProperty("flutter.sdk")
            require(flutterSdkPath != null) { "flutter.sdk not set in local.properties" }
            flutterSdkPath
        }

    includeBuild("$flutterSdkPath/packages/flutter_tools/gradle")

    repositories {
        google()
        mavenCentral()
        gradlePluginPortal()
    }
}

plugins {
    id("dev.flutter.flutter-plugin-loader") version "1.0.0"
    id("com.android.application") version "9.3.1" apply false
    id("org.jetbrains.kotlin.android") version "2.4.20" apply false
}

include(":app")

====================================================================================================
FILE: ios\Runner\AppDelegate.swift
====================================================================================================
import Flutter
import UIKit

@main
@objc class AppDelegate: FlutterAppDelegate, FlutterImplicitEngineDelegate {
  override func application(
    _ application: UIApplication,
    didFinishLaunchingWithOptions launchOptions: [UIApplication.LaunchOptionsKey: Any]?
  ) -> Bool {
    return super.application(application, didFinishLaunchingWithOptions: launchOptions)
  }

  func didInitializeImplicitFlutterEngine(_ engineBridge: FlutterImplicitEngineBridge) {
    GeneratedPluginRegistrant.register(with: engineBridge.pluginRegistry)
  }
}

FILE: ios\Runner\Assets.xcassets\LaunchImage.imageset\README.md

# Launch Screen Assets

You can customize the launch screen with your own desired assets by replacing the image files in this directory.

You can also do it by opening your Flutter project's Xcode project with `open ios/Runner.xcworkspace`, selecting `Runner/Assets.xcassets` in the Project Navigator and dropping in the desired images.

                ),

  }

  void _selectRole(SihamaRole role) {
    setState(() {
      _role = role;
      _emailController.text = role.demoEmail;
      _sampleDocumentSelected = false;
    });
  }

  void _submit() {
    if (!_formKey.currentState!.validate()) return;
    widget.onAuthenticated(_role);
  }

  Widget _fieldLabel(String text) => Text(
    text,
    style: const TextStyle(
      color: _forest,
      fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
    ),
  );
}

InputDecoration _decoration(String hint) => InputDecoration(
  hintText: hint,
  filled: true,
  fillColor: Colors.white,
  prefixIconColor: _muted,
  contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 15, vertical: 14),
  border: OutlineInputBorder(
    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
    borderSide: const BorderSide(color: Color(0xFFE0E7E1)),
  ),
  enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
    borderSide: const BorderSide(color: Color(0xFFE0E7E1)),
  ),
  focusedBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
    borderSide: const BorderSide(color: _green, width: 1.5),
  ),
);

String? _required(String? value) =>
    value == null || value.trim().isEmpty ? 'Bagian ini wajib diisi.' : null;


====================================================================================================
FILE: lib\data\report_image.dart
====================================================================================================
import 'dart:typed_data';

import 'package:file_picker/file_picker.dart';

const maxReportImageBytes = 5 * 1024 * 1024;
const allowedReportImageExtensions = ['png', 'jpg', 'jpeg'];

class ReportImage {
  const ReportImage({required this.name, required this.bytes});

  final String name;
  final Uint8List bytes;
}

typedef ReportImagePicker = Future<ReportImage?> Function();

class ReportImagePickerException implements Exception {
  const ReportImagePickerException(this.message);

  final String message;

  @override
  String toString() => message;
}

Future<ReportImage?> pickReportImage() async {
  final files = await FilePicker.pickFiles(
    dialogTitle: 'Pilih foto tanaman',
    type: FileType.custom,
    allowedExtensions: allowedReportImageExtensions,
  );
  if (files.isEmpty) return null;
  final file = files.first;
  final size = await file.length();
  if (size != null && size > maxReportImageBytes) {
    throw const ReportImagePickerException('Ukuran foto maksimal 5 MB.');
  }
  return ReportImage(name: file.name, bytes: await file.readAsBytes());
}

String? validateReportImage(ReportImage image) {
  final extension = image.name.split('.').last.toLowerCase();
  if (!allowedReportImageExtensions.contains(extension)) {
    return 'Format foto harus PNG, JPG, atau JPEG.';
  }
  if (image.bytes.isEmpty) {
    return 'File gambar kosong atau tidak dapat dibaca.';
  }
  if (image.bytes.length > maxReportImageBytes) {
    return 'Ukuran foto maksimal 5 MB.';
  }

  final isPng =
      image.bytes.length >= 8 &&
      image.bytes[0] == 0x89 &&
      image.bytes[1] == 0x50 &&
      image.bytes[2] == 0x4E &&
      image.bytes[3] == 0x47 &&
      image.bytes[4] == 0x0D &&
      image.bytes[5] == 0x0A &&
      image.bytes[6] == 0x1A &&
      image.bytes[7] == 0x0A;
  final isJpeg =
      image.bytes.length >= 3 &&
      image.bytes[0] == 0xFF &&
      image.bytes[1] == 0xD8 &&
      image.bytes[2] == 0xFF;

  if ((extension == 'png' && !isPng) ||
      ((extension == 'jpg' || extension == 'jpeg') && !isJpeg)) {
    return 'Isi file tidak sesuai dengan format gambar yang dipilih.';
  }
  return null;
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: lib\data\sihama_database.dart
====================================================================================================
import 'dart:convert';

import 'package:path/path.dart' as path;
import 'package:sqflite/sqflite.dart';

import 'sihama_repository.dart';

class SihamaDatabase implements SihamaRepository {
  SihamaDatabase();

  Database? _database;

  Future<Database> get _db async {
    if (_database != null) return _database!;

    final databasePath = path.join(await getDatabasesPath(), 'sihama.db');
    _database = await openDatabase(
      databasePath,
      version: 7,
      onCreate: (db, version) async {
        await db.execute('''
          CREATE TABLE reports (
            id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
            crop TEXT NOT NULL,
            location TEXT NOT NULL,
            description TEXT NOT NULL,
            date TEXT NOT NULL,
            status TEXT NOT NULL,
            photo_bytes BLOB,
            diagnosis TEXT,
            recommendation TEXT
          )
        ''');
        await db.execute('''
          CREATE TABLE products (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            name TEXT NOT NULL,
            category TEXT NOT NULL,
            price INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (price >= 0),
            stock INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (stock >= 0),
            description TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
            photo_bytes BLOB
          )
        ''');
        await db.execute(_marketOrdersTable);
        await db.execute('''
          CREATE TABLE farmer_profiles (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK (id = 1),
            name TEXT NOT NULL,
            role TEXT NOT NULL,
            region TEXT NOT NULL,
            phone TEXT NOT NULL,
            address TEXT NOT NULL
          )
        ''');

        final batch = db.batch();
        batch.insert('reports', {
          'id': 'SIH-240618',
          'crop': 'Padi sawah',
          'location': 'Petak Utara',
          'description': 'Daun menguning di beberapa bagian.',
          'date': DateTime(2025, 6, 18, 8, 24).toIso8601String(),
          'status': 'Diajukan',
        });
        batch.insert('products', {
          'name': 'Kocide 54 WDG',
          'category': 'Bakterisida',
          'price': 85000,
          'stock': 42,
        });
        batch.insert('products', {
          'name': 'Nordox 56 WP',
          'category': 'Bakterisida',
          'price': 72000,
          'stock': 4,
        });
        batch.insert('products', {
          'name': 'Score 250 EC',
          'category': 'Fungisida',
          'price': 142000,
          'stock': 18,
        });
        batch.insert('products', {
          'name': 'Dithane M-45 80 WP',
          'category': 'Fungisida',
          'price': 68000,
          'stock': 3,
        });
        _addSampleOrders(batch);
        batch.insert('farmer_profiles', {
          'id': 1,
          'name': 'Budi Santoso',
          'role': 'Petani',
          'region': 'Kabupaten Bogor',
          'phone': '',
          'address': '',
        });
        await batch.commit(noResult: true);
      },
      onUpgrade: (db, oldVersion, newVersion) async {
        if (oldVersion < 2) {
          await db.execute(
            'ALTER TABLE products ADD COLUMN stock INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (stock >= 0)',
          );
          await db.update(
            'farmer_profiles',
            {'name': 'Budi Santoso'},
            where: 'id = ? AND name = ?',
            whereArgs: [1, 'Pak Budi'],
          );
        }
        if (oldVersion < 3) {
          await db.execute('ALTER TABLE reports ADD COLUMN photo_bytes BLOB');
        }
        if (oldVersion < 4) {
          await db.execute(_marketOrdersTable);
          final batch = db.batch();
          _addSampleOrders(batch);
          await batch.commit(noResult: true);
        }
        if (oldVersion < 5) {
          await db.execute('ALTER TABLE reports ADD COLUMN diagnosis TEXT');
          await db.execute(
            'ALTER TABLE reports ADD COLUMN recommendation TEXT',
          );
        }
        if (oldVersion >= 4 && oldVersion < 6) {
          await db.execute(
            'ALTER TABLE market_orders ADD COLUMN transfer_proof_bytes BLOB',
          );
        }
        if (oldVersion < 7) {
          await db.execute(
            "ALTER TABLE products ADD COLUMN description TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''",
          );
          await db.execute('ALTER TABLE products ADD COLUMN photo_bytes BLOB');
        }
      },
    );
    return _database!;
  }

  @override
  Future<void> initialize() async {
    await _db;
  }

  @override
  Future<List<CropReport>> getReports() async {
    final rows = await (await _db).query('reports', orderBy: 'date DESC');
    return rows.map(CropReport.fromMap).toList();
  }

  @override
  Future<void> createReport(CropReport report) async {
    await (await _db).insert('reports', report.toMap());
  }

  @override
  Future<void> updateReport(CropReport report) async {
    final changed = await (await _db).update(
      'reports',
      report.toMap(),
      where: 'id = ?',
      whereArgs: [report.id],
    );
    _requireUpdated(changed, 'Laporan');
  }

  @override
  Future<void> deleteReport(String id) async {
    final deleted = await (await _db).delete(
      'reports',
      where: 'id = ?',
      whereArgs: [id],
    );
    _requireUpdated(deleted, 'Laporan');
  }

  @override
  Future<List<MarketProduct>> getProducts() async {
    final rows = await (await _db).query(
      'products',
      orderBy: 'name COLLATE NOCASE',
    );
    return rows.map(MarketProduct.fromMap).toList();
  }

  @override
  Future<int> createProduct(MarketProduct product) async {
    return (await _db).insert('products', product.toMap());
  }

  @override
  Future<void> updateProduct(MarketProduct product) async {
    final id = product.id;
    if (id == null) throw ArgumentError.value(id, 'product.id');
    final changed = await (await _db).update(
      'products',
      product.toMap(),
      where: 'id = ?',
      whereArgs: [id],
    );
    _requireUpdated(changed, 'Produk');
  }

  @override
  Future<void> deleteProduct(int id) async {
    final deleted = await (await _db).delete(
      'products',
      where: 'id = ?',
      whereArgs: [id],
    );
    _requireUpdated(deleted, 'Produk');
  }

  @override
  Future<List<MarketOrder>> getOrders() async {
    final rows = await (await _db).query('market_orders', orderBy: 'date DESC');
    return rows.map(MarketOrder.fromMap).toList();
  }

  @override
  Future<void> createOrder(MarketOrder order) async {
    if (order.items.isEmpty) {
      throw ArgumentError.value(order.items, 'order.items');
    }
    await (await _db).transaction((txn) async {
      for (final item in order.items) {
        if (item.quantity <= 0) {
          throw ArgumentError.value(item.quantity, 'item.quantity');
        }
        final changed = await txn.rawUpdate(
          'UPDATE products SET stock = stock - ? WHERE id = ? AND stock >= ?',
          [item.quantity, item.productId, item.quantity],
        );
        if (changed != 1) {
          throw StateError(
            'Stok ${item.productName} tidak mencukupi untuk pesanan.',
          );
        }
      }
      await txn.insert('market_orders', order.toMap());
    });
  }

  @override
  Future<void> updateOrder(MarketOrder order) async {
    final db = await _db;
    await db.transaction((txn) async {
      final rows = await txn.query(
        'market_orders',
        where: 'id = ?',
        whereArgs: [order.id],
        limit: 1,
      );
      if (rows.isEmpty) throw StateError('Pesanan tidak ditemukan.');
      final current = MarketOrder.fromMap(rows.first.cast<String, Object?>());
      if (current.status == order.status) return;
      if (!_canTransitionOrder(current.status, order.status)) {
        throw StateError(
          'Status pesanan tidak dapat diubah dari ${current.status} menjadi ${order.status}.',
        );
      }
      if (order.status == 'Dibatalkan' && current.status != 'Dibatalkan') {
        for (final item in current.items) {
          await txn.rawUpdate(
            'UPDATE products SET stock = stock + ? WHERE id = ?',
            [item.quantity, item.productId],
          );
        }
      }
      final changed = await txn.update(
        'market_orders',
        order.toMap(),
        where: 'id = ?',
        whereArgs: [order.id],
      );
      _requireUpdated(changed, 'Pesanan');
    });
  }

  @override
  Future<FarmerProfile?> getProfile() async {
    final rows = await (await _db).query(
      'farmer_profiles',
      where: 'id = ?',
      whereArgs: [1],
      limit: 1,
    );
    return rows.isEmpty ? null : FarmerProfile.fromMap(rows.first);
  }

  @override
  Future<void> createProfile(FarmerProfile profile) async {
    await (await _db).insert('farmer_profiles', profile.toMap());
  }

  @override
  Future<void> updateProfile(FarmerProfile profile) async {
    final changed = await (await _db).update(
      'farmer_profiles',
      profile.toMap(),
      where: 'id = ?',
      whereArgs: [profile.id],
    );
    _requireUpdated(changed, 'Profil');
  }

  @override
  Future<void> deleteProfile(int id) async {
    final deleted = await (await _db).delete(
      'farmer_profiles',
      where: 'id = ?',
      whereArgs: [id],
    );
    _requireUpdated(deleted, 'Profil');
  }

  void _requireUpdated(int count, String entity) {
    if (count != 1) {
      throw StateError('$entity tidak ditemukan atau sudah dihapus.');
    }
  }

  static const _marketOrdersTable = '''
    CREATE TABLE market_orders (
      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
      customer_name TEXT NOT NULL,
      date TEXT NOT NULL,
      status TEXT NOT NULL,
      items TEXT NOT NULL,
      total INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (total >= 0),
      transfer_proof_bytes BLOB
    )
  ''';

  static void _addSampleOrders(Batch batch) {
    final samples = [
      (

        DateTime(2025, 5, 10),
        'Selesai',

          {

            'unit_price': 68000,
          },
        ],
        68000,
      ),
      (

        'Dikirim',
        [
          {
            'product_id': 1,
            'product_name': 'Kocide 54 WDG',
            'quantity': 3,
            'unit_price': 85000,
          },
        ],
        255000,
      ),
      (
        'P-2207',
        'Sutrisno',
        DateTime(2025, 5, 16),
        'Dipesan',
        [
          {
            'product_id': 3,
            'product_name': 'Score 250 EC',
            'quantity': 1,
            'unit_price': 142000,
          },
          {
            'product_id': 4,
            'product_name': 'Dithane M-45 80 WP',
            'quantity': 2,
            'unit_price': 68000,
          },
        ],
        278000,

      (

        'Budi Santoso',
        DateTime(2025, 5, 13),
        'Diproses',
        [
          {
            'product_id': 2,
            'product_name': 'Nordox 56 WP',
            'quantity': 2,
            'unit_price': 72000,
          },
        ],
        144000,
      ),
    ];
    for (final sample in samples) {
      batch.insert('market_orders', {
        'id': sample.$1,
        'customer_name': sample.$2,
        'date': sample.$3.toIso8601String(),
        'status': sample.$4,
        'items': jsonEncode(sample.$5),
        'total': sample.$6,
      });
    }
  }

  static bool _canTransitionOrder(String current, String next) {
    if (current == next) return true;
    if (next == 'Dibatalkan') {
      return current == 'Dipesan' || current == 'Diproses';
    }
    return switch (current) {
      'Dipesan' => next == 'Diproses',
      'Diproses' => next == 'Dikirim',
      'Dikirim' => next == 'Selesai',
      _ => false,
    };
  }
}

import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:typed_data';

class CropReport {
  const CropReport({
    required this.id,
    required this.crop,
    required this.location,
    required this.description,
    required this.date,
    required this.status,
    this.photoBytes,
    this.diagnosis,
    this.recommendation,
  });

  final String id;
  final String crop;
  final String location;
  final String description;
  final DateTime date;
  final String status;
  final Uint8List? photoBytes;
  final String? diagnosis;
  final String? recommendation;

  CropReport copyWith({
    String? status,
    String? diagnosis,
    String? recommendation,
  }) => CropReport(
    id: id,
    crop: crop,
    location: location,
    description: description,
    date: date,
    status: status ?? this.status,
    photoBytes: photoBytes,
    diagnosis: diagnosis ?? this.diagnosis,
    recommendation: recommendation ?? this.recommendation,
  );

  Map<String, Object?> toMap() => {
    'id': id,
    'crop': crop,
    'location': location,
    'description': description,
    'date': date.toIso8601String(),
    'status': status,
    'photo_bytes': photoBytes,
    'diagnosis': diagnosis,
    'recommendation': recommendation,
  };

  factory CropReport.fromMap(Map<String, Object?> map) {
    final photo = map['photo_bytes'];
    return CropReport(
      id: map['id']! as String,
      crop: map['crop']! as String,
      location: map['location']! as String,
      description: map['description']! as String,
      date: DateTime.parse(map['date']! as String),
      status: map['status']! as String,
      photoBytes: photo is Uint8List
          ? photo
          : photo is List<int>
          ? Uint8List.fromList(photo)
          : null,
      diagnosis: map['diagnosis'] as String?,
      recommendation: map['recommendation'] as String?,
    );
  }
}

class MarketProduct {
  const MarketProduct({
    this.id,
    required this.name,
    required this.category,
    required this.price,
    this.stock = 0,
    this.description = '',
    this.photoBytes,
  });

  final int? id;
  final String name;
  final String category;
  final int price;
  final int stock;
  final String description;
  final Uint8List? photoBytes;

  Map<String, Object?> toMap() => {
    if (id != null) 'id': id,
    'name': name,
    'category': category,
    'price': price,
    'stock': stock,
    'description': description,
    'photo_bytes': photoBytes,
  };

  factory MarketProduct.fromMap(Map<String, Object?> map) {
    final photo = map['photo_bytes'];
    return MarketProduct(
      id: map['id']! as int,
      name: map['name']! as String,
      category: map['category']! as String,
      price: map['price']! as int,
      stock: (map['stock'] as int?) ?? 0,
      description: (map['description'] as String?) ?? '',
      photoBytes: photo is Uint8List
          ? photo
          : photo is List<int>
          ? Uint8List.fromList(photo)
          : null,
    );
  }
}

class FarmerProfile {
  const FarmerProfile({
    this.id = 1,
    required this.name,
    required this.role,
    required this.region,
    required this.phone,
    required this.address,
  });

  final int id;
  final String name;
  final String role;
  final String region;
  final String phone;
  final String address;

  Map<String, Object?> toMap() => {
    'id': id,
    'name': name,
    'role': role,
    'region': region,
    'phone': phone,
    'address': address,
  };

  factory FarmerProfile.fromMap(Map<String, Object?> map) => FarmerProfile(
    id: map['id']! as int,
    name: map['name']! as String,
    role: map['role']! as String,
    region: map['region']! as String,
    phone: map['phone']! as String,
    address: map['address']! as String,
  );
}

class MarketOrderItem {
  const MarketOrderItem({
    required this.productId,
    required this.productName,
    required this.quantity,
    required this.unitPrice,
  });

  final int productId;
  final String productName;
  final int quantity;
  final int unitPrice;

  int get total => quantity * unitPrice;

  Map<String, Object?> toMap() => {
    'product_id': productId,
    'product_name': productName,
    'quantity': quantity,
    'unit_price': unitPrice,
  };

  factory MarketOrderItem.fromMap(Map<String, Object?> map) => MarketOrderItem(
    productId: map['product_id']! as int,
    productName: map['product_name']! as String,
    quantity: map['quantity']! as int,
    unitPrice: map['unit_price']! as int,
  );
}

class MarketOrder {
  const MarketOrder({
    required this.id,
    required this.customerName,
    required this.date,
    required this.status,
    required this.items,
    this.transferProofBytes,
  });

  final String id;
  final String customerName;
  final DateTime date;
  final String status;
  final List<MarketOrderItem> items;
  final Uint8List? transferProofBytes;

  int get total => items.fold(0, (sum, item) => sum + item.total);

  MarketOrder copyWith({String? status}) => MarketOrder(
    id: id,
    customerName: customerName,
    date: date,
    status: status ?? this.status,
    items: items,
    transferProofBytes: transferProofBytes,
  );

  Map<String, Object?> toMap() => {
    'id': id,
    'customer_name': customerName,
    'date': date.toIso8601String(),
    'status': status,
    'items': jsonEncode(items.map((item) => item.toMap()).toList()),
    'total': total,
    'transfer_proof_bytes': transferProofBytes,
  };

  factory MarketOrder.fromMap(Map<String, Object?> map) {
    final decodedItems = jsonDecode(map['items']! as String) as List<dynamic>;
    return MarketOrder(
      id: map['id']! as String,
      customerName: map['customer_name']! as String,
      date: DateTime.parse(map['date']! as String),
      status: map['status']! as String,
      items: decodedItems
          .map(
            (item) =>
                MarketOrderItem.fromMap(Map<String, Object?>.from(item as Map)),
          )
          .toList(),
      transferProofBytes: map['transfer_proof_bytes'] is Uint8List
          ? map['transfer_proof_bytes'] as Uint8List
          : map['transfer_proof_bytes'] is List<int>
          ? Uint8List.fromList(map['transfer_proof_bytes'] as List<int>)
          : null,
    );
  }
}

abstract interface class SihamaRepository {
  Future<void> initialize();
  Future<List<CropReport>> getReports();
  Future<void> createReport(CropReport report);
  Future<void> updateReport(CropReport report);
  Future<void> deleteReport(String id);
  Future<List<MarketProduct>> getProducts();
  Future<int> createProduct(MarketProduct product);
  Future<void> updateProduct(MarketProduct product);
  Future<void> deleteProduct(int id);
  Future<List<MarketOrder>> getOrders();
  Future<void> createOrder(MarketOrder order);
  Future<void> updateOrder(MarketOrder order);
  Future<FarmerProfile?> getProfile();
  Future<void> createProfile(FarmerProfile profile);
  Future<void> updateProfile(FarmerProfile profile);
  Future<void> deleteProfile(int id);
}

enum SihamaRole {
  pelapor('Pelapor', 'budi@sihama.id'),
  penyuluh('Penyuluh', 'siti@sihama.id'),
  mitraToko('Mitra Toko', 'tanimakmur@sihama.id');

  const SihamaRole(this.label, this.demoEmail);

  final String label;
  final String demoEmail;
}

import 'package:material_ui/material_ui.dart';

import 'auth_screen.dart';
import 'data/report_image.dart';
import 'data/sihama_repository.dart';
import 'data/sihama_role.dart';
import 'sihama_shell.dart';

void main() {
  runApp(const SihamaApp());
}

class SihamaApp extends StatefulWidget {
  const SihamaApp({super.key, this.repository, this.imagePicker});

  final SihamaRepository? repository;
  final ReportImagePicker? imagePicker;

  @override
  State<SihamaApp> createState() => _SihamaAppState();
}

class _SihamaAppState extends State<SihamaApp> {
  SihamaRole? _role;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return MaterialApp(
      title: 'SIHAMA',
      debugShowCheckedModeBanner: false,
      theme: ThemeData(
        useMaterial3: true,
        colorScheme: ColorScheme.fromSeed(seedColor: const Color(0xFF1E5A46)),
        scaffoldBackgroundColor: const Color(0xFFF7F9F6),
      ),
      home: _role == null
          ? AuthScreen(onAuthenticated: (role) => setState(() => _role = role))
          : SihamaShell(
              key: ValueKey(_role),
              repository: widget.repository,
              imagePicker: widget.imagePicker,
              role: _role!,
              onLogout: () => setState(() => _role = null),
            ),
    );
  }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: lib\sihama_shell.dart
====================================================================================================
import 'package:material_ui/material_ui.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';

import 'data/report_image.dart';
import 'data/sihama_database.dart';
import 'data/sihama_repository.dart';
import 'data/sihama_role.dart';

const _forest = Color(0xFF173F2D);
const _green = Color(0xFF285E4D);
const _ink = Color(0xFF183B2E);
const _muted = Color(0xFF5E6F68);
const _surface = Color(0xFFF3F7F4);
const _unspecifiedCrop = 'Tanaman';

class SihamaShell extends StatefulWidget {
  const SihamaShell({
    super.key,
    this.repository,
    this.imagePicker,
    this.role = SihamaRole.pelapor,
    this.onLogout,
  });

  final SihamaRepository? repository;
  final ReportImagePicker? imagePicker;
  final SihamaRole role;
  final VoidCallback? onLogout;

  @override
  State<SihamaShell> createState() => _SihamaShellState();
}

class _SihamaShellState extends State<SihamaShell> {
  int _selectedIndex = 0;
  bool _showReportForm = false;
  bool _showConversations = false;
  late final SihamaRepository _repository =
      widget.repository ?? SihamaDatabase();
  List<CropReport> _reports = [];
  List<MarketProduct> _products = [];
  List<MarketOrder> _orders = [];
  FarmerProfile? _profile;
  bool _loading = true;
  Object? _loadError;

  bool get _hasReviewedReport =>
      _reports.any((report) => report.status == 'Selesai Ditinjau');

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _loadData();
  }

  Future<void> _loadData({bool showLoading = true}) async {
    if (showLoading && mounted) setState(() => _loading = true);
    try {
      await _repository.initialize();
      final reports = await _repository.getReports();
      final products = await _repository.getProducts();
      final orders = await _repository.getOrders();
      final profile = await _repository.getProfile();
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _reports = reports;
        _products = products;
        _orders = orders;
        _profile = profile;
        _loadError = null;
        _loading = false;
      });
    } catch (error) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _loadError = error;
        _loading = false;
      });
    }
  }

  Future<bool> _runMutation(
    String successMessage,
    Future<void> Function() action,
  ) async {
    try {
      await action();
      await _loadData(showLoading: false);
      if (!mounted) return false;
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
          .showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(successMessage)));
      return true;
    } catch (error) {
      if (!mounted) return false;
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        SnackBar(content: Text('Gagal menyimpan perubahan: $error')),
      );
      return false;
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (_loading || _loadError != null) {
      return Scaffold(
        backgroundColor: _surface,
        body: SafeArea(
          child: Column(
            children: [
              _TopBar(onNotifications: _openNotifications),
              Expanded(
                child: Center(
                  child: _loading
                      ? const CircularProgressIndicator()
                      : Padding(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
                          child: Column(
                            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                            children: [
                              const Icon(
                                Icons.storage_rounded,
                                size: 40,
                                color: _green,
                              ),
                              const SizedBox(height: 12),
                              const Text(
                                'Data SIHAMA gagal dimuat.',
                                style: TextStyle(
                                  fontSize: 17,
                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                                  color: _ink,
                                ),
                              ),
                              const SizedBox(height: 8),
                              Text(
                                '$_loadError',
                                textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                                style: const TextStyle(color: _muted),
                              ),
                              const SizedBox(height: 16),
                              FilledButton(
                                onPressed: _loadData,
                                child: const Text('Coba lagi'),
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      );
    }

    final reviewedReports = _reports
        .where((report) => report.status == 'Selesai Ditinjau')
        .toList();
    final pages = <Widget>[
      if (widget.role == SihamaRole.pelapor)
        _HomeTab(
          latestReport: _reports.isEmpty ? null : _reports.first,
          reviewedReport: reviewedReports.isEmpty
              ? null
              : reviewedReports.first,
          onCreateReport: () => setState(() {
            _selectedIndex = 1;
            _showReportForm = true;
            _showConversations = false;
          }),
          onOpenHistory: () => setState(() {
            _selectedIndex = 1;
            _showReportForm = false;
            _showConversations = false;
          }),
          onOpenReport: (report) => _showReportDetails(context, report),
          onOpenConversations: () => setState(() => _showConversations = true),
        ),
      if (widget.role == SihamaRole.pelapor)
        _showReportForm
            ? _ReportTab(
                imagePicker: widget.imagePicker ?? pickReportImage,
                onCancel: () => setState(() => _showReportForm = false),
                onSubmit: (report) async {
                  final saved = await _runMutation(
                    'Laporan berhasil dikirim ke antrean penyuluh.',
                    () => _repository.createReport(report),
                  );
                  if (saved && mounted) {
                    setState(() {
                      _selectedIndex = 1;
                      _showReportForm = false;
                    });
                  }
                  return saved;
                },
              )
            : _HistoryTab(
                reports: _reports,
                onCreateReport: () => setState(() => _showReportForm = true),
                onOpenReport: (report) => _showReportDetails(context, report),
                onEdit: _editReport,
                onDelete: _deleteReport,
              ),
      if (widget.role == SihamaRole.penyuluh)
        _HistoryTab(
          reports: _reports,
          title: 'Laporan Masuk',
          subtitle: 'Tinjau laporan petani dan perbarui status pemeriksaan.',
          onOpenReport: (report) => _showReportDetails(context, report),
          onEdit: _editReport,
          onDelete: _deleteReport,
        ),
      if (widget.role == SihamaRole.penyuluh) const _DiseaseDatabaseTab(),
      if (widget.role == SihamaRole.mitraToko)
        _ShopOrdersTab(orders: _orders, onUpdateOrder: _updateOrder),
      if (widget.role != SihamaRole.penyuluh)
        _MarketTab(
          products: _products,
          canOrder: _hasReviewedReport,
          canManage: widget.role == SihamaRole.mitraToko,
          imagePicker: widget.imagePicker ?? pickReportImage,
          onCheckout: _checkoutOrder,
          onChangeStock: _changeProductStock,
          onCreate: _createProduct,
          onEdit: _editProduct,
          onDelete: _deleteProduct,
        ),
      _NotificationsTab(
        role: widget.role,
        reports: _reports,
        orders: _orders,
        customerName: _profile?.name ?? 'Petani SIHAMA',
        onOpenReport: (report) => _showReportDetails(context, report),
        onOpenOrder: (order) => _showOrderDetails(context, order),
        onOpenChat: () => Navigator.of(context)
            .push(MaterialPageRoute<void>(builder: (_) => const _ChatScreen())),
      ),
      _ProfileTab(
        profile: _profile,
        role: widget.role,
        onEdit: _editProfile,
        onDelete: _deleteProfile,
        onLogout: widget.onLogout,
        reportCount: _reports.length,
        orderCount: widget.role == SihamaRole.mitraToko
            ? _orders.length
            : _orders
                  .where((order) => order.customerName == _profile?.name)
                  .length,
      ),
    ];
    if (widget.role == SihamaRole.pelapor &&
        _selectedIndex == 0 &&
        _showConversations) {
      pages[0] = _ConversationTab(
        onBack: () => setState(() => _showConversations = false),
      );
    }

    final navigationItems = switch (widget.role) {
      SihamaRole.pelapor => _BottomNavigation.reporterItems,
      SihamaRole.penyuluh => _BottomNavigation.adviserItems,
      SihamaRole.mitraToko => _BottomNavigation.shopItems,
    };

    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: _surface,
      body: SafeArea(
        child: IndexedStack(index: _selectedIndex, children: pages),
      ),
      bottomNavigationBar: _showConversations
          ? null
          : _BottomNavigation(
              items: navigationItems,
              selectedIndex: _selectedIndex,
              onSelected: (index) => setState(() => _selectedIndex = index),
            ),
    );
  }

  void _showReportDetails(BuildContext context, CropReport report) {
    if (widget.role == SihamaRole.penyuluh) {
      Navigator.of(context).push(
        MaterialPageRoute<void>(
          builder: (_) => _AdviserReportScreen(
            report: report,
            onSave: (updated) => _runMutation(
              'Diagnosis laporan ${report.id} berhasil disimpan.',
              () => _repository.updateReport(updated),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      );
      return;
    }
    Navigator.of(context).push(
      MaterialPageRoute<void>(
        builder: (context) => Scaffold(
          backgroundColor: _surface,
          appBar: AppBar(
            backgroundColor: Colors.white,
            foregroundColor: _ink,
            elevation: 0,
            titleSpacing: 0,
            title: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(
                  'Laporan ${report.id}',
                  style: const TextStyle(
                    fontSize: 17,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
                  ),
                ),
                Text(
                  '${_reportTitle(report)} · ${_formatDate(report.date)}',
                  style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: _muted),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          body: ListView(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 12, 16, 24),
            children: [
              if (report.photoBytes != null) ...[
                ClipRRect(
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                  child: Image.memory(
                    report.photoBytes!,
                    height: 190,
                    width: double.infinity,
                    fit: BoxFit.cover,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
              ] else
                Container(
                  height: 160,
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                    gradient: const LinearGradient(
                      colors: [Color(0xFFD7F0D3), Color(0xFFB8DF9B)],
                    ),
                  ),
                  child: const Icon(
                    Icons.eco_outlined,
                    size: 38,
                    color: _green,
                  ),
                ),
              const SizedBox(height: 12),
              Row(
                children: [
                  Expanded(child: _StatusBadge(status: report.status)),
                  Text(
                    'Pelapor: ${_profile?.name ?? 'Petani SIHAMA'}',
                    style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: _muted),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 12),
              _DetailCard(
                title: 'Gejala dilaporkan',
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Text(
                      report.description,
                      style: const TextStyle(color: _ink, height: 1.45),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 10),
                    Text(
                      'Tanggal laporan: ${_formatDate(report.date)}',
                      style: const TextStyle(color: _muted, fontSize: 12),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              if (report.status == 'Diajukan' ||
                  report.status == 'Ditinjau') ...[
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: const Color(0xFFFFF3D5),
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
                  ),
                  child: const Row(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      Icon(Icons.schedule_rounded, color: Color(0xFF946313)),
                      SizedBox(width: 10),
                      Expanded(
                        child: Text(
                          'Menunggu peninjauan penyuluh. Anda akan menerima notifikasi setelah laporan selesai ditinjau.',
                          style: TextStyle(
                            color: Color(0xFF72500E),
                            height: 1.4,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ] else ...[
                const SizedBox(height: 12),
                _DetailCard(
                  title: 'Diagnosis Penyuluh',
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      Text(
                        report.diagnosis ??
                            'Ikuti arahan petugas dan pantau kondisi tanaman secara berkala.',
                        style: const TextStyle(color: _ink, height: 1.45),
                      ),
                      if (report.recommendation?.isNotEmpty == true) ...[
                        const SizedBox(height: 8),
                        Text(
                          report.recommendation!,
                          style: const TextStyle(color: _muted, height: 1.4),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  void _showOrderDetails(BuildContext context, MarketOrder order) {
    Navigator.of(context).push(
      MaterialPageRoute<void>(builder: (_) => _OrderDetailScreen(order: order)),
    );
  }

  Future<void> _editReport(CropReport report) async {
    final updated = await showDialog<CropReport>(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => _ReportFormDialog(report: report),
    );
    if (updated != null) {
      await _runMutation(
        'Laporan berhasil diperbarui.',
        () => _repository.updateReport(updated),
      );
    }
  }

  Future<void> _deleteReport(CropReport report) async {
    if (!await _confirmDelete(context, 'laporan ${report.id}')) return;
    await _runMutation(
      'Laporan berhasil dihapus.',
      () => _repository.deleteReport(report.id),
    );
  }

  Future<void> _createProduct() async {
    final product = await Navigator.of(context).push<MarketProduct>(
      MaterialPageRoute<MarketProduct>(
        builder: (_) => _ProductFormScreen(
          imagePicker: widget.imagePicker ?? pickReportImage,
        ),
      ),
    );
    if (product != null) {
      await _runMutation('Produk berhasil ditambahkan.', () async {
        await _repository.createProduct(product);
      });
    }
  }

  Future<bool> _checkoutOrder(
    List<MarketOrderItem> items,
    ReportImage paymentProof,
  ) async {
    final now = DateTime.now();
    final order = MarketOrder(
      id: 'P-${now.microsecondsSinceEpoch}',
      customerName: _profile?.name ?? 'Petani SIHAMA',
      date: now,
      status: 'Dipesan',
      items: items,
      transferProofBytes: paymentProof.bytes,
    );
    return _runMutation(
      'Pesanan ${order.id} berhasil dibuat.',
      () => _repository.createOrder(order),
    );
  }

  Future<bool> _changeProductStock(MarketProduct product, int stock) {
    return _runMutation('Stok ${product.name} diperbarui.', () {
      return _repository.updateProduct(MarketProduct(
        id: product.id,
        name: product.name,
        category: product.category,
        price: product.price,
        stock: stock,
        description: product.description,
        photoBytes: product.photoBytes,
      ));
    });
  }

  Future<bool> _updateOrder(MarketOrder order, String status) async {
    return _runMutation(
      'Status pesanan ${order.id} diperbarui.',
      () => _repository.updateOrder(order.copyWith(status: status)),
    );
  }

  Future<void> _editProduct(MarketProduct product) async {
    final updated = await Navigator.of(context).push<MarketProduct>(
      MaterialPageRoute<MarketProduct>(
        builder: (_) => _ProductFormScreen(
          product: product,
          imagePicker: widget.imagePicker ?? pickReportImage,
        ),
      ),
    );
    if (updated != null) {
      await _runMutation(
        'Produk berhasil diperbarui.',
        () => _repository.updateProduct(updated),
      );
    }
  }

  Future<void> _deleteProduct(MarketProduct product) async {
    final id = product.id;
    if (id == null ||
        !await _confirmDelete(context, 'produk ${product.name}')) {
      return;
    }
    await _runMutation(
      'Produk berhasil dihapus.',
      () => _repository.deleteProduct(id),
    );
  }

  Future<void> _saveProfile(FarmerProfile profile) async {
    await _runMutation(
      _profile == null
          ? 'Profil berhasil dibuat.'
          : 'Profil berhasil diperbarui.',
      () => _profile == null
          ? _repository.createProfile(profile)
          : _repository.updateProfile(profile),
    );
  }

  Future<void> _editProfile() async {
    final profile = await showDialog<FarmerProfile>(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => _ProfileFormDialog(profile: _profile),
    );
    if (profile != null) await _saveProfile(profile);
  }

  Future<void> _deleteProfile() async {
    final profile = _profile;
    if (profile == null ||
        !await _confirmDelete(context, 'profil ${profile.name}')) {
      return;
    }
    await _runMutation(
      'Profil berhasil dihapus.',
      () => _repository.deleteProfile(profile.id),
    );
  }

  void _openNotifications() {
    setState(() {
      _selectedIndex = switch (widget.role) {
        SihamaRole.pelapor => 3,
        SihamaRole.penyuluh || SihamaRole.mitraToko => 2,
      };
    });
  }
}

  const _TopBar({required this.onNotifications});

  final VoidCallback onNotifications;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      width: double.infinity,
      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 12, 16, 12),

      child: Row(

class _BottomNavigation extends StatelessWidget {
  const _BottomNavigation({
    required this.items,
    required this.selectedIndex,
    required this.onSelected,
  });

  final List<({String label, IconData icon})> items;
  final int selectedIndex;
  final ValueChanged<int> onSelected;

  static const reporterItems = [
    (label: 'Beranda', icon: Icons.home_rounded),
    (label: 'Laporan', icon: Icons.assignment_outlined),
    (label: 'AgriMarket', icon: Icons.storefront_rounded),
    (label: 'Notifikasi', icon: Icons.notifications_none_rounded),
    (label: 'Profil', icon: Icons.person_rounded),

  static const adviserItems = [
    (label: 'Laporan Masuk', icon: Icons.assignment_outlined),
    (label: 'Basis Data', icon: Icons.storage_rounded),

  @override

          BoxShadow(
            color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: 0.05),

            offset: const Offset(0, -2),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      child: SafeArea(
        top: false,
        child: Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(8, 7, 8, 8),
          child: Row(
            children: List.generate(items.length, (index) {
              final item = items[index];
              final selected = index == selectedIndex;
              return Expanded(
                child: Semantics(
                  button: true,
                  selected: selected,
                  label: item.label,
                  child: GestureDetector(
                    onTap: () => onSelected(index),

                      decoration: BoxDecoration(

                        color: selected
                            ? const Color(0xFFE7F2ED)
                            : Colors.transparent,
                      ),
                      child: Column(
                        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                        children: [

                            Stack(
                              clipBehavior: Clip.none,
                              children: [
                                Icon(
                                  item.icon,
                                  size: 21,

                                    ),

                                      '2',
                                      style: TextStyle(
                                        color: Colors.white,
                                        fontSize: 9,
                                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ],
                            )
                          else
                            Icon(
                              item.icon,
                              size: 21,
                              color: selected
                                  ? _green
                                  : const Color(0xFF6F7D74),
                            ),

                            item.label,

                        ],

class _HomeReportCard extends StatelessWidget {
  const _HomeReportCard({required this.report, required this.onTap});

  final CropReport report;
  final VoidCallback onTap;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Material(
      color: Colors.white,
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
      child: InkWell(
        onTap: onTap,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
        child: Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
          child: Row(
            children: [
              Container(
                width: 48,
                height: 48,
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: const Color(0xFFD8F0D5),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(11),
                ),
                child: const Icon(Icons.eco_outlined, color: _green),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 10),
              Expanded(
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Text(
                      report.crop == _unspecifiedCrop
                          ? report.id
                          : '${report.crop} · ${report.id}',
                      style: const TextStyle(
                        color: _ink,
                        fontSize: 12,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 4),
                    _StatusBadge(status: report.status),
                    const SizedBox(height: 4),
                    Text(
                      'Indikasi awal: ${_initialDiagnosis(report.crop)}',
                      style: const TextStyle(color: _muted, fontSize: 10),
                    ),
                    Text(
                      '${report.status == 'Selesai Ditinjau' ? 'Ditinjau penyuluh' : 'Menunggu penyuluh'} · ${_formatDate(report.date).split(' - ').first}',
                      style: const TextStyle(color: _muted, fontSize: 10),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

String _initialDiagnosis(String crop) => switch (crop.toLowerCase()) {
  'cabai' => 'Antraknosa',
  'tomat' => 'Layu Fusarium',
  _ => 'Hawar daun',
};

String _reportTitle(CropReport report) => report.crop == _unspecifiedCrop
    ? report.location
    : '${report.crop} - ${report.location}';

}

class _ChatScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const _ChatScreen();

  @override
  State<_ChatScreen> createState() => _ChatScreenState();
}

class _ChatScreenState extends State<_ChatScreen> {
  final _controller = TextEditingController();
  final _messages = <(String, bool)>[
    (
      'Jika hujan dalam 2 jam setelah semprot, ulangi penyemprotan. Ringankan petakan 3–4 hari dulu ya.',
      false,
    ),
    ('Baik, terima kasih Bu. Saya pantau dulu.', true),
    (
      'Bercak terlihat sudah tidak melebar. Lanjutkan jadwal semprot dan kurangi pupuk urea.',
      false,
    ),
  ];

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _controller.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _send() {
    final value = _controller.text.trim();
    if (value.isEmpty) return;
    setState(() {
      _messages.add((value, true));
      _controller.clear();
    });
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: const Color(0xFFF0F4F1),
      appBar: AppBar(
        backgroundColor: Colors.white,
        foregroundColor: _ink,
        elevation: 0,
        titleSpacing: 0,
        title: const Row(
          children: [
            CircleAvatar(
              radius: 16,
              backgroundColor: Color(0xFFD8F0D5),
              child: Text('SR', style: TextStyle(color: _green, fontSize: 11)),
            ),
            SizedBox(width: 10),
            Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(
                  'Ir. Siti Rahmawati',
                  style: TextStyle(fontSize: 15, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800),
                ),
                Text(
                  '●  Sesi aktif',
                  style: TextStyle(fontSize: 10, color: _muted),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
      body: Column(
        children: [
          Container(
            width: double.infinity,
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 8),
            color: Colors.white,
            child: Row(
              children: [
                const Icon(Icons.eco_outlined, color: _green),
                const SizedBox(width: 8),
                const Expanded(
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      Text(
                        'L-1042 · Padi · Hawar Daun Bakteri',
                        maxLines: 1,
                        overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                        style: TextStyle(
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                          fontSize: 11,
                        ),
                      ),
                      Text(
                        'Selesai Ditinjau',
                        style: TextStyle(fontSize: 10, color: _green),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
                TextButton(
                  onPressed: () {},
                  child: const Text(
                    'Ubah gejala',
                    style: TextStyle(fontSize: 10),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          Expanded(
            child: ListView(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
              children: [
                const Center(
                  child: Chip(
                    label: Text('Hari ini', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 10)),
                  ),
                ),
                ..._messages.map(
                  (message) => Align(
                    alignment: message.$2
                        ? Alignment.centerRight
                        : Alignment.centerLeft,
                    child: Container(
                      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 8),
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                      constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 280),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: message.$2
                            ? const Color(0xFF25833E)
                            : Colors.white,
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
                      ),
                      child: Text(
                        message.$1,
                        style: TextStyle(
                          color: message.$2 ? Colors.white : _ink,
                          height: 1.4,
                          fontSize: 12,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          SafeArea(
            top: false,
            child: Container(
              color: Colors.white,
              padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(12, 8, 12, 8),
              child: Row(
                children: [
                  IconButton(
                    onPressed: () {},
                    icon: const Icon(
                      Icons.add_photo_alternate_outlined,
                      color: _green,
                    ),
                  ),
                  Expanded(
                    child: TextField(
                      controller: _controller,
                      onSubmitted: (_) => _send(),
                      decoration: InputDecoration(
                        hintText: 'Tulis pesan...',
                        isDense: true,
                        contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                          horizontal: 14,
                          vertical: 11,
                        ),
                        border: OutlineInputBorder(
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(24),
                          borderSide: const BorderSide(
                            color: Color(0xFFE3EAE5),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                  IconButton(
                    onPressed: _send,
                    icon: const Icon(Icons.send_rounded, color: _green),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

}

class _OrderDetailScreen extends StatelessWidget {
  const _OrderDetailScreen({required this.order});
  final MarketOrder order;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final steps = ['Dipesan', 'Diproses', 'Dikirim', 'Selesai'];
    final active = steps.indexOf(order.status).clamp(0, steps.length - 1);
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: _surface,
      appBar: AppBar(
        backgroundColor: Colors.white,
        foregroundColor: _ink,
        elevation: 0,
        title: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Text(
              'Pesanan ${order.id}',
              style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 17, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800),
            ),
            Text(
              'Toko Tani Makmur · ${_formatDate(order.date)}',
              style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: _muted),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
      body: ListView(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 14, 16, 24),
        children: [
          const Text(
            'Status',
            style: TextStyle(color: _ink, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: Colors.white,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
              border: Border.all(color: const Color(0xFFE4EBE5)),
            ),
            child: Column(
              children: [
                Align(
                  alignment: Alignment.centerRight,
                  child: _StatusBadge(status: order.status),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 8),
                ...List.generate(steps.length, (index) {
                  final reached = index <= active;
                  return Row(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      SizedBox(
                        width: 28,
                        child: Column(
                          children: [
                            Container(
                              width: 22,
                              height: 22,
                              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                color: reached
                                    ? const Color(0xFF2A9148)
                                    : const Color(0xFFE4EBE5),
                                shape: BoxShape.circle,
                              ),
                              child: Icon(
                                index < active
                                    ? Icons.check_rounded
                                    : Icons.circle,
                                size: 12,
                                color: Colors.white,
                              ),
                            ),
                            if (index < steps.length - 1)
                              Container(
                                width: 2,
                                height: 28,
                                color: index < active
                                    ? const Color(0xFF2A9148)
                                    : const Color(0xFFE4EBE5),
                              ),
                          ],
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(width: 8),
                      Padding(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 4),
                        child: Text(
                          steps[index],
                          style: TextStyle(
                            color: reached ? _ink : _muted,
                            fontSize: 12,
                            fontWeight: index == active
                                ? FontWeight.w800
                                : FontWeight.w500,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  );
                }),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          _DetailCard(
            title: 'Rincian pesanan',
            child: Column(
              children: [
                ...order.items.map(
                  (item) => Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 10),
                    child: Row(
                      children: [
                        Expanded(
                          child: Text(
                            '${item.productName} × ${item.quantity}',
                            style: const TextStyle(color: _muted, fontSize: 12),
                          ),
                        ),
                        Text(
                          _formatPrice(item.total),
                          style: const TextStyle(
                            color: _ink,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                            fontSize: 12,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
                const Divider(),
                Row(
                  mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                  children: [
                    const Text(
                      'Total',
                      style: TextStyle(
                        color: _ink,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                      ),
                    ),
                    Text(
                      _formatPrice(order.total),
                      style: const TextStyle(
                        color: Color(0xFF18834A),
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _AdviserReportScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const _AdviserReportScreen({required this.report, required this.onSave});

  final CropReport report;
  final Future<bool> Function(CropReport report) onSave;

  @override
  State<_AdviserReportScreen> createState() => _AdviserReportScreenState();
}

class _AdviserReportScreenState extends State<_AdviserReportScreen> {
  static const _diseases = [
    ('Hawar Daun Bakteri', 'Xanthomonas oryzae pv. oryzae'),
    ('Hawar Daun Pyricularia', 'Pyricularia oryzae'),
    ('Blas', 'Pyricularia oryzae'),
    ('Antraknosa', 'Colletotrichum capsici'),
    ('Layu Fusarium', 'Fusarium oxysporum'),
  ];

  late String? _diagnosis = widget.report.diagnosis;
  late final TextEditingController _recommendationController =
      TextEditingController(text: widget.report.recommendation ?? '');
  bool _saving = false;

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _recommendationController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _save() async {
    final diagnosis = _diagnosis;
    if (diagnosis == null || _recommendationController.text.trim().isEmpty) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(content: Text('Pilih diagnosis dan isi rekomendasi.')),
      );
      return;
    }
    setState(() => _saving = true);
    final saved = await widget.onSave(
      widget.report.copyWith(
        status: 'Selesai Ditinjau',
        diagnosis: diagnosis,
        recommendation: _recommendationController.text.trim(),
      ),
    );
    if (!mounted) return;
    setState(() => _saving = false);
    if (saved) Navigator.of(context).pop();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => Scaffold(
    backgroundColor: _surface,
    appBar: AppBar(
      backgroundColor: Colors.white,
      foregroundColor: _ink,
      elevation: 0,
      title: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
        const Text('Tinjau & Diagnosis', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 17, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
        Text('${widget.report.crop} · ${_formatDate(widget.report.date)}', style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 11, color: _muted)),
      ]),
    ),
    body: ListView(padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 12, 16, 24), children: [
      if (widget.report.photoBytes != null)
        ClipRRect(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(15), child: Image.memory(widget.report.photoBytes!, height: 180, fit: BoxFit.cover))
      else
        Container(height: 150, decoration: BoxDecoration(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(15), gradient: const LinearGradient(colors: [Color(0xFFD7F0D3), Color(0xFFB8DF9B)])), child: const Icon(Icons.eco_outlined, size: 38, color: _green)),
      const SizedBox(height: 12),
      Row(children: [Expanded(child: Text('Laporan ${widget.report.id} · ${widget.report.location}', style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: _ink, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700))), _StatusBadge(status: widget.report.status)]),
      const SizedBox(height: 12),
      _DetailCard(title: 'Gejala dilaporkan', child: Text(widget.report.description, style: const TextStyle(color: _ink, height: 1.45))),
      const SizedBox(height: 14),
      const Text('Jenis hama/penyakit', style: TextStyle(color: _ink, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
      const SizedBox(height: 8),
      ..._diseases.map((disease) {
        final selected = _diagnosis == disease.$1;
        return InkWell(
          onTap: () => setState(() => _diagnosis = disease.$1),
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(13),
          child: Container(
            margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 8),
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(color: Colors.white, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(13), border: Border.all(color: selected ? _green : const Color(0xFFE2E9E3), width: selected ? 1.5 : 1)),
            child: Row(children: [Icon(selected ? Icons.radio_button_checked : Icons.radio_button_unchecked, color: selected ? _green : _muted, size: 20), const SizedBox(width: 9), Expanded(child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [Text(disease.$1, style: const TextStyle(color: _ink, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, fontSize: 12)), Text(disease.$2, style: const TextStyle(color: _muted, fontSize: 10))]))]),
          ),
        );
      }),
      const SizedBox(height: 6),
      const Text('Rekomendasi penanganan', style: TextStyle(color: _ink, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
      const SizedBox(height: 8),
      TextField(controller: _recommendationController, maxLines: 4, decoration: _inputDecoration('Tulis rekomendasi yang akan diterima petani')),
      const SizedBox(height: 16),
      SizedBox(height: 48, child: FilledButton.icon(onPressed: _saving ? null : _save, icon: _saving ? const SizedBox(width: 18, height: 18, child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2, color: Colors.white)) : const Icon(Icons.check_rounded), label: Text(_saving ? 'Menyimpan...' : 'Simpan Diagnosis'))),
    ]),
  );
}

  @override

class _HistoryTab extends StatefulWidget {
  const _HistoryTab({
    required this.reports,
    required this.onOpenReport,
    required this.onEdit,
    required this.onDelete,
    this.title = 'Riwayat laporan',
    this.subtitle = 'Pantau perkembangan laporan tanaman Anda.',
    this.onCreateReport,
  });

  final List<CropReport> reports;
  final ValueChanged<CropReport> onOpenReport;
  final ValueChanged<CropReport> onEdit;
  final ValueChanged<CropReport> onDelete;
  final String title;
  final String subtitle;
  final VoidCallback? onCreateReport;

  @override
  State<_HistoryTab> createState() => _HistoryTabState();
}

class _HistoryTabState extends State<_HistoryTab> {
  _HistoryFilter _filter = _HistoryFilter.all;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final reports = widget.reports.where((report) {
      return switch (_filter) {
        _HistoryFilter.all => true,
        _HistoryFilter.inProgress => report.status != 'Selesai Ditinjau',
        _HistoryFilter.reviewed => report.status == 'Selesai Ditinjau',
      };
    }).toList();

    return ListView(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(18, 22, 18, 28),
      children: [
        Row(
          children: [
            Expanded(
              child: Text(
                widget.title,
                style: const TextStyle(
                  color: _ink,
                  fontSize: 19,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
                ),
              ),
            ),
            if (widget.onCreateReport != null)
              IconButton.filled(
                onPressed: widget.onCreateReport,
                style: IconButton.styleFrom(
                  backgroundColor: _green,
                  foregroundColor: Colors.white,
                ),
                icon: const Icon(Icons.add_rounded),
                tooltip: 'Buat laporan',
              ),
          ],
        ),
        if (widget.subtitle.isNotEmpty && widget.title == 'Laporan Masuk')
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 2),
            child: Text(
              widget.subtitle,
              style: const TextStyle(color: _muted, fontSize: 12),
            ),
          ),
        const SizedBox(height: 14),
        SingleChildScrollView(
          scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
          child: Row(
            children: _HistoryFilter.values.map((filter) {
              final selected = filter == _filter;
              return Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 8),
                child: InkWell(
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(999),
                  onTap: () => setState(() => _filter = filter),
                  child: Container(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                      horizontal: 14,
                      vertical: 9,
                    ),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: selected ? _green : Colors.white,
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(999),
                      border: Border.all(
                        color: selected
                            ? const Color(0xFFDCEDE4)
                            : const Color(0xFFE5E8E4),
                      ),
                    ),
                    child: Text(
                      filter.label,
                      style: TextStyle(
                        color: selected ? Colors.white : _muted,
                        fontWeight: selected
                            ? FontWeight.w700
                            : FontWeight.w500,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              );
            }).toList(),
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 12),
        if (reports.isEmpty)
          const _EmptyState(
            icon: Icons.assignment_outlined,
            title: 'Belum ada laporan',
            message: 'Laporan dengan status ini akan muncul di sini.',
          )
        else
          ...reports.map(
            (report) => Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
              child: _ReportCard(
                report: report,
                onTap: () => widget.onOpenReport(report),
                onEdit: () => widget.onEdit(report),
                onDelete: () => widget.onDelete(report),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        const SizedBox(height: 6),
        const _PrototypeNote(),
      ],
    );
  }
}

class _ReportTab extends StatefulWidget {
  const _ReportTab({
    required this.imagePicker,
    required this.onSubmit,
    required this.onCancel,
  });

  final ReportImagePicker imagePicker;
  final Future<bool> Function(CropReport report) onSubmit;
  final VoidCallback onCancel;

  @override
  State<_ReportTab> createState() => _ReportTabState();
}

  final _locationController = TextEditingController();
  final _descriptionController = TextEditingController();

    'Daun menguning dari tepi',
    'Bercak memanjang kering',

    'Daun menggulung',

    'Buah membusuk',

    _descriptionController.dispose();
    super.dispose();

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Form(
      key: _formKey,

                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,

                      ),

                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 16, 16, 28),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Row(
                  children: [
                    const _FormLabel('Foto tanaman'),
                    const SizedBox(width: 4),
                    Text(
                      '*wajib',
                      style: TextStyle(
                        color: Colors.red.shade700,
                        fontSize: 12,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 6),
                InkWell(
                  onTap: _pickImage,
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
                  child: Container(
                    width: double.infinity,
                    height: 108,

                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),

                            ? const Color(0xFF25833E)

                      ),
                    ),
